Numerical tables online

(this page is part of the LOCOMAT project)

The following list is an attempt to organize the growing number of digitized and reconstructed computed tables available on the web. There are currently links to more than 4000 books containing computed tables, some of the books being only table-books.

The list is organized by categories, and I have at the moment only put in separate lists a number of collection of ephemerides. In the future, I plan to better organize this list, in particular displaying differently the numerous reprints (especially in the 20th century), and identifying the tabular contents.

Please, help me to complete this list. Send me missing links at roegel(at)gmail.com. All digitized mathematical, astronomical, and in general, computed, tables should be included. Many thanks to all those who already provided entries! If you want your name listed, let me know.
